##### Raw second transcription

```yaml
```yaml
lines:
  - id: person.ira_box1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Wages, salaries, tips, etc. Generally, this is the amount reported in box 1 of Form W-2 (this person's Forms W-2)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: w2, field: w2.box1}
    note: "Read as this person's share of IRA Deduction Worksheet line 8: the total of box 1 of the person's Forms W-2."

  - id: person.h_ira_limit_std
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$7,000 if under age 50 at the end of 2025"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ira.limit}

  - id: person.h_ira_limit_50
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$8,000 if age 50 or older at the end of 2025"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ira.limit_50}

  - id: person.h_ira_limit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Applicable amount: $7,000 if under age 50 at the end of 2025; $8,000 if age 50 or older at the end of 2025"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.age50_at_year_end, {const: true}]}
          then: person.h_ira_limit_50
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.age50_at_year_end, {const: false}]}
          then: person.h_ira_limit_std

  - id: person.h_ira_ssb_a
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Smaller of the applicable amount (line 7 when lines 2 through 6 are skipped) or line 10"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.h_ira_limit, ira.line10]}

  - id: person.ira_ssb
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "IRA deduction when no one is covered by a retirement plan: smallest of line 7 (the applicable amount), line 10, or line 11"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.h_ira_ssb_a, person.ira_trad]}
    note: "The IRS pages do not name this result. It is read as the IRA deduction figured without lines 2 through 6, because nobody is covered by a retirement plan. In that case the deduction does not depend on modified AGI, so the Social Security Benefits Worksheet can use it. When someone is covered and contributions were made, the line 6a/6b Exception sends the filer to Pub. 590-A, which is screened by ssb.ira_covered."

  - id: person.h_ira_skip
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Checked No on line 1a (and No on line 1b if married filing jointly): skip lines 2 through 6"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [ira.line1a, {const: false}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, ne: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [ira.line1b, {const: false}]}

  - id: person.h_ira_covered
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "The person for this column was covered by a retirement plan (line 1a for your IRA, line 1b for your spouse's IRA)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [person.role, {const: "taxpayer"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [ira.line1a, {const: true}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [person.role, {const: "spouse"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [ira.line1b, {const: true}]}

  - id: person.h_ira_line2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 2 for this person's column (2a for your IRA, 2b for your spouse's IRA)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.role, {const: "taxpayer"}]}
          then: ira.line2a
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.role, {const: "spouse"}]}
          then: ira.line2b

  - id: person.h_ira_line6
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Subtract line 5 from line 2 in this column"
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: person.h_ira_line2, take: ira.line5}

  - id: person.h_ira_line6_pos
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 6 for this column, not less than zero"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: person.h_ira_line2, take: ira.line5}

  - id: person.h_ira_stop
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Is the amount on line 5 less than the amount on line 2? No: none of your IRA contributions are deductible"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, le: [person.h_ira_line6, {const: 0}]}
    note: "Line 6 says 'None of your IRA contributions are deductible' without saying whether this applies to each column or to both. On a joint return where one spouse is not covered, the two columns have different line 2 amounts ($146,000 and $246,000). The test is applied to each column separately."

  - id: person.h_ira_narrow
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Single, head of household, or married filing separately, or the column for the IRA of a person who wasn't covered by a retirement plan"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "single"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "hoh"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfs"}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.h_ira_covered, {const: false}]}

  - id: person.h_ira_thr_narrow
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$10,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ira.full_narrow}

  - id: person.h_ira_thr_joint
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$20,000"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ira.full_joint}

  - id: person.h_ira_thr
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 6 amount at or above which the full applicable amount is entered on line 7"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_ira_narrow, {const: true}]}
          then: person.h_ira_thr_narrow
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_ira_narrow, {const: false}]}
          then: person.h_ira_thr_joint

  - id: person.h_ira_full
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "The line 6 result is $10,000 or more ($20,000 or more if married filing jointly or qualifying surviving spouse, other than the column of a person who wasn't covered)"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, ge: [person.h_ira_line6, person.h_ira_thr]}

  - id: person.h_ira_p70
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 6 multiplied by 70% (0.70)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: person.h_ira_line6_pos, rate: {param: ira.rate_70}}

  - id: person.h_ira_p80
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 6 multiplied by 80% (0.80)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: person.h_ira_line6_pos, rate: {param: ira.rate_80}}

  - id: person.h_ira_p35
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 6 multiplied by 35% (0.35)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: person.h_ira_line6_pos, rate: {param: ira.rate_35}}

  - id: person.h_ira_p40
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 6 multiplied by 40% (0.40)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: multiply_rate, amount: person.h_ira_line6_pos, rate: {param: ira.rate_40}}

  - id: person.h_ira_p
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Multiply line 6 by the percentage that applies to you"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [person.h_ira_narrow, {const: true}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [person.age50_at_year_end, {const: true}]}
          then: person.h_ira_p80
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [person.h_ira_narrow, {const: true}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [person.age50_at_year_end, {const: false}]}
          then: person.h_ira_p70
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [person.h_ira_narrow, {const: false}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [person.age50_at_year_end, {const: true}]}
          then: person.h_ira_p40
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [person.h_ira_narrow, {const: false}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [person.age50_at_year_end, {const: false}]}
          then: person.h_ira_p35

  - id: person.h_ira_p_up
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "If the result isn't a multiple of $10, increase it to the next multiple of $10"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: round, of: person.h_ira_p, unit: 1000, rule: up}

  - id: person.h_ira_min
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "$200"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ira.min}

  - id: person.h_ira_line7_red
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "If the result is $200 or more, enter the result. But if it is less than $200, enter $200"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: larger_of, of: [person.h_ira_p_up, person.h_ira_min]}

  - id: person.h_ira_line7_cov
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Line 7 for this column when lines 2 through 6 are completed"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_ira_stop, {const: true}]}
          then: person.zero
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [person.h_ira_stop, {const: false}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [person.h_ira_full, {const: true}]}
          then: person.h_ira_limit
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [person.h_ira_stop, {const: false}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [person.h_ira_full, {const: false}]}
          then: person.h_ira_line7_red

  - id: person.h_ira_ded_cov_a
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Smaller of line 7 or line 10"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.h_ira_line7_cov, ira.line10]}

  - id: person.h_ira_ded_cov
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Smallest of line 7, 10, or 11 when lines 2 through 6 are completed"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [person.h_ira_ded_cov_a, person.ira_trad]}

  - id: person.ira_deduction
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "On line 12a, enter the smallest of line 7a, 10, or 11a. On line 12b, enter the smallest of line 7b, 10, or 11b. This is the most you can deduct."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_ira_skip, {const: true}]}
          then: person.ira_ssb
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.h_ira_skip, {const: false}]}
          then: person.h_ira_ded_cov
    refuses: [ira.kbh_short, ira.both_kinds]
    note: "The worksheet column is chosen by person.role: your IRA for the taxpayer, your spouse's IRA for the spouse. Line 11 is this person's traditional IRA contributions (person.ira_trad). The option to deduct a smaller amount is not encoded; the maximum is used. The refusals stand for two cases the text sends to Pub. 590-A: the caution when married filing jointly and line 10 is below $14,000/$15,000/$16,000, and item 2 (joint return with contributions to both traditional and Roth IRAs). They are read from the catalog gate names."

  - id: person.h_ira_nd_a
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Smaller of line 10 or line 11 of the IRA Deduction Worksheet"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [ira.line10, person.ira_trad]}

  - id: person.ira_nondeductible
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8606 line 1: subtract line 12 of the IRA Deduction Worksheet from the smaller of line 10 or line 11 of the worksheet"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: person.h_ira_nd_a, take: person.ira_deduction}
    refuses: [person.ira_extra_left, person.ira_recharacterized]
    note: "When no contribution is deductible, the instructions say nondeductible contributions may be made up to the contribution limit. The same formula is used there. Contributions above the limit that were not withdrawn are screened by person.ira_extra_left (item 10, special rules in Pub. 590-A)."

  - id: person.ira_8606_line2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8606 line 2: Enter your total basis in traditional IRAs"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: person.ira_basis}
    refuses: [person.ira_basis_adjusted]
    note: "The supplied input gives the Total Basis Chart amount, or zero for a first-time filer. Changes to basis from returned excess contributions, a transfer incident to divorce, or a rollover of a nontaxable part are not computed, so the line refuses when they occurred."

  - id: person.ira_files_8606
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "File 2025 Form 8606: nondeductible contributions to a traditional IRA for 2025, or distributions from a traditional IRA in 2025 with basis in these IRAs more than zero"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, gt: [person.ira_nondeductible, {const: 0}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [person.ira_dist, {const: true}]}
            - {op: test, gt: [person.ira_8606_line2, {const: 0}]}
    note: "Only the filing tests the supplied inputs can express are encoded. A Roth conversion with zero basis and Roth IRA distributions (Part III) also require Form 8606. The person.ira_dist question cannot separate conversions from distributions, and no input covers Roth distributions."

  - id: person.ira_dist_gate
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Form 8606: In 2025, did you take a distribution from a traditional IRA, or make a Roth IRA conversion? (Yes: go to line 4)"
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [person.ira_files_8606, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [person.ira_dist, {const: true}]}
    note: "Read as the Yes answer to the question after Form 8606 line 3, asked only when this person files Form 8606."

  - id: person.h_edu_adj_sum
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Adjusted Qualified Education Expenses Worksheet line 3: Total adjustments (add lines 2a, 2b, and 2c)"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [person.edu_a_this_year, person.edu_a_next_year, person.edu_refunds]}

  - id: person.edu_adj
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Total adjustments (tax-free educational assistance and refunds) for this student"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.edu, {const: true}]}
          then: person.h_edu_adj_sum
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [person.edu, {const: false}]}
          then: person.zero
    note: "Zero when nobody paid qualified education expenses for this person as a student, since the adjustment inputs are not asked then."

  - id: person.x_tax
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Additional tax on IRAs or other tax-favored accounts (Form 5329) for this person"
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: f5329, field: f5329.tax}
    note: "Read as the total of this person's Form 5329 additional taxes, which feed Schedule 2 line 8."
```

Second transcription's note: The worksheet column is chosen by person.role: your IRA for the taxpayer, your spouse's IRA for the spouse. Line 11 is this person's traditional IRA contributions (person.ira_trad). The option to deduct a smaller amount is not encoded; the maximum is used. The refusals stand for two cases the text sends to Pub. 590-A: the caution when married filing jointly and line 10 is below $14,000/$15,000/$16,000, and item 2 (joint return with contributions to both traditional and Roth IRAs). They are read from the catalog gate names.

Second transcription's note: When no contribution is deductible, the instructions say nondeductible contributions may be made up to the contribution limit. The same formula is used there. Contributions above the limit that were not withdrawn are screened by person.ira_extra_left (item 10, special rules in Pub. 590-A).

Second transcription's note: The IRS pages do not name this result. It is read as the IRA deduction figured without lines 2 through 6, because nobody is covered by a retirement plan. In that case the deduction does not depend on modified AGI, so the Social Security Benefits Worksheet can use it. When someone is covered and contributions were made, the line 6a/6b Exception sends the filer to Pub. 590-A, which is screened by ssb.ira_covered.
